    def _role_file(root: Path, role: str) -> tuple[str, bytes, list[str]]:
        """The role file's path (relative to ``root``), bytes and lines; refused when it cannot be read."""
        roles_rel = ADOPTED_ROLES_REL if _adopted(root) else ROLES_REL
        rel = f"{roles_rel}/{role}.md"
        try:
            data = (root / rel).read_bytes()
            return rel, data, data.decode("utf-8").splitlines()
        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
>           raise _refuse(f"the role file {rel} cannot be read: the session would start without its role (DEC-826 Q1)") \
                from None

src/gov/launch/role.py:231: GovError
_ test_s19_the_slots_refuse_a_launch_only_when_their_reading_cannot_be_taken[heavy_slots missing-a worker] _
[gw5] linux -- Python 3.12.3 /usr/bin/python3

machine = <w2_20_support.Machine object at 0x7ed434b4ed50>
launch = <function launch.<locals>._launch at 0x7ed43581e020>, role = 'engineer'
ticket = 'DAEO-wrk01', planted = 'heavy_slots missing'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("role, ticket", ROLES, ids=ROLE_IDS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("planted", list(SLOTS_UNREADABLE))
    def test_s19_the_slots_refuse_a_launch_only_when_their_reading_cannot_be_taken(machine, launch, role, ticket,
                                                                                    planted):
        """P-2 (c), settled (DEC-1097 (8)): a launch is refused on the slots only when their reading cannot be taken;
        the refusal names ``heavy_slots``."""
        from gov.cli.errors import GovError
    
        values = machine.config_values()
        if SLOTS_UNREADABLE[planted] is None:
            del values["heavy_slots"]
        else:
            values["heavy_slots"] = SLOTS_UNREADABLE[planted]
        machine.write_config(values)
        with pytest.raises(GovError) as refused:

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("role, ticket", ROLES, ids=ROLE_IDS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("planted", list(SLOTS_UNREADABLE))
    def test_s19_the_slots_refuse_a_launch_only_when_their_reading_cannot_be_taken(machine, launch, role, ticket,
                                                                                    planted):
        """P-2 (c), settled (DEC-1097 (8)): a launch is refused on the slots only when their reading cannot be taken;
        the refusal names ``heavy_slots``."""
        from gov.cli.errors import GovError
    
        values = machine.config_values()
        if SLOTS_UNREADABLE[planted] is None:
            del values["heavy_slots"]
        else:
            values["heavy_slots"] = SLOTS_UNREADABLE[planted]
        machine.write_config(values)
        with pytest.raises(GovError) as refused:
            launch(role, ticket)
        assert refused.value.code == "LAUNCH_REFUSED", refused.value

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["gov resources --check", "a launch"])
    def test_r2_f1_expired_server_samples_are_taken_afresh_so_the_first_reading_opens_on_cpu(
            machine, repo, monkeypatch, request, path):
        """Both servers present and idle, their kept samples all three hours old: the first reading opens on cpu (the
        start check opens; the launch starts its session), the reading shows both servers' current use as a number, and
        the samples file then holds a sample of each server taken by that reading."""
        launch = request.getfixturevalue("launch") if path == "a launch" else None  # noqa: F811
        machine.located(repo)  # the suite's location check first (a plain reading on planted figures), never after
        servers = _idle_servers(machine)
        hosts = {server["setting"]: server["host"] for server in servers.values()}
        unplanted = {"GOV_RESOURCES_EMBEDDING_CPU": None, "GOV_RESOURCES_EMBEDDING_CPU_NOW": None}
        _plant_expired_history(machine, servers)
        began = time.time()
    
        if path == "gov resources --check":
            code, envelope, out, err = machine.check(repo, env_extra={**unplanted, **hosts})
            if envelope is not None and envelope.get("ok") is False:
                reasons = support.reasons_by_condition(support.refusal_of(code, envelope, out, err))
                pytest.fail(f"the first reading on an expired history shut the gate: {reasons}")
            reading = support.opened_of(code, envelope, out, err)
            now = reading.get("embedding_cpu_now")
            assert isinstance(now, dict), f"the servers' current use is not shown after their samples expired: {reading}"
            for name in servers:
                value = now.get(name)
                assert isinstance(value, (int, float)) and 0.0 <= value <= IDLE_MAX_CORES, (
                    f"the {name} server is idle, but its current use reads {value!r}: {now}")
            p95 = reading.get("embedding_cpu_p95")
            assert isinstance(p95, dict) and all(isinstance(p95.get(n), (int, float)) for n in servers), (
                f"the servers' p95 is not a reading after their samples expired: {p95}")
        else:
            from gov.cli.errors import GovError
    
            for key in unplanted:
                monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)
            for key, value in hosts.items():
                monkeypatch.setenv(key, value)
            try:

    def _role_file(root: Path, role: str) -> tuple[str, bytes, list[str]]:
        """The role file's path (relative to ``root``), bytes and lines; refused when it cannot be read."""
        roles_rel = ADOPTED_ROLES_REL if _adopted(root) else ROLES_REL
        rel = f"{roles_rel}/{role}.md"
        try:
            data = (root / rel).read_bytes()
            return rel, data, data.decode("utf-8").splitlines()

    def _role_file(root: Path, role: str) -> tuple[str, bytes, list[str]]:
        """The role file's path (relative to ``root``), bytes and lines; refused when it cannot be read."""
        roles_rel = ADOPTED_ROLES_REL if _adopted(root) else ROLES_REL
        rel = f"{roles_rel}/{role}.md"
        try:
            data = (root / rel).read_bytes()
            return rel, data, data.decode("utf-8").splitlines()
        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):

repo_root = PosixPath('/home/usain/gov-os-worktrees/W2-60a')

    def test_the_integration_merges_of_this_repository_pass(repo_root):
        """Every merge commit on HEAD's history that touches tests/acceptance/** passes the DEC-253 rule.
    
        Rewrite (W2-70, DEC-908 item 3): ``merge_offences`` reads the path map's
        ``acceptance_exemptions`` (DEC-906), so a branch made before the listed
        ``c9a08678`` may merge the line that holds it back.
        """
        merges = [sha for sha in support.commits_touching(ACCEPTANCE, repo_root)
                  if len(support.commit_parents(sha, repo_root)) > 1
                  and DESIGNER not in support.commit_roles(sha, repo_root)]
        assert merges, "no merge commit on HEAD's history touches tests/acceptance/**: 00e3d539 is expected there"
        failing = {sha[:12]: support.merge_offences(sha, ACCEPTANCE, repo_root) for sha in merges}
        failing = {sha: why for sha, why in failing.items() if why}
>       assert not failing, f"integration merges on HEAD's history do not pass the DEC-253 rule: {failing}"
E       AssertionError: integration merges on HEAD's history do not pass the DEC-253 rule: {'5b7941b66aa2': ['the merge commit itself changes tests/acceptance/W2-50/w2_50_support.py, tests/acceptance/W2-58/README.md, tests/acceptance/W2-58/test_w2_58_launched_sessions.py, tests/acceptance/W2-58/w2_58_support.py']}

add = ['add', '-A']

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("add", (["add", "-A"], ["add", "."]), ids=("git-add-A", "git-add-dot"))
    def test_s4_no_placeholder_is_staged_or_committed_inside_the_session(box, add):
        s.set_plan(box, [
            {"do": "plant", "names": list(s.PLACEHOLDERS)},
            *[{"do": "write", "path": rel, "text": "work\n"} for rel in WORK],

tests/acceptance/W2-56/test_w2_56_placeholders.py:54: AssertionError
__ test_f1_launches_started_together_admit_only_those_the_memory_rule_admits ___
[gw3] linux -- Python 3.12.3 /usr/bin/python3

planted = <conftest.Machine object at 0x7e64d7cb5910>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x7e64d7cb46e0>

    def test_f1_launches_started_together_admit_only_those_the_memory_rule_admits(planted, monkeypatch):
        """Six launches at once against room for three (floor 3 GiB, working size 2 GiB, 9 GiB available): only three
        sessions start, and the other three are refused on memory. Each check is slowed after its reading, so without
        the lock all six would read no session and start."""
        monkeypatch.setenv("GOV_RESOURCES_PROC", "/proc")  # real sessions: their pids are alive
        project = _quiet_launch_checks(planted, monkeypatch)
        planted.config(memory_floor_bytes=3 * GIB, working_size_bytes=2 * GIB)
        planted.memory(available=9 * GIB)  # 9 + the stand-ins' few MiB: n=2 needs 9 (opens), n=3 needs 11 (shuts)
        marks = planted.root / "marks"
        marks.mkdir()
        cli = planted.home / launcher.CLI_REL
        cli.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        # The folder is written into the stand-in: the launcher passes the CLI a cleaned environment (W2-56 S5), so a
        # variable of this process's would not reach it.
        cli.write_text(STAND_IN_CLI.replace("#!/bin/sh\n", f"#!/bin/sh\nMARKS={shlex.quote(str(marks))}\n", 1))
        cli.chmod(0o755)
        real_check = launch_gate.check_start
    
        def slow_check(**kw):
            reading = real_check(**kw)
            time.sleep(0.3)  # the window between the reading and the registration, widened
            return reading
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(launch_gate, "check_start", slow_check)
        children = []
        try:
            for i in range(6):
                pid = os.fork()
                if pid == 0:  # pragma: no cover - the child
                    code = 4
                    try:
                        launcher.launch(project, "engineer", f"T-{i}", [])
                        code = 0
                    except GovError as error:
                        (marks / f"refused-{i}").write_text(error.message)
                        code = 3
                    except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
                        (marks / f"error-{i}").write_text(repr(error))
                    finally:
                        os._exit(code)
                children.append(pid)
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 60
            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                settled = _count(marks, "started-") + _count(marks, "refused-") + _count(marks, "error-")
                entries = len(list((planted.dir / "sessions").glob("*.json"))) if (planted.dir / "sessions").exists() else 0
                if settled == 6 and entries == _count(marks, "started-"):
                    break
                time.sleep(0.05)
            assert _count(marks, "error-") == 0, [p.read_text() for p in marks.glob("error-*")]
>           assert _count(marks, "started-") == 3 and _count(marks, "refused-") == 3
E           AssertionError: assert (0 == 3)
E            +  where 0 = _count(PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-60a/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw3/test_f1_launches_started_toget0/marks'), 'started-')

    def test_f1_the_check_runs_under_the_machine_wide_start_lock(planted, monkeypatch):
        project = _quiet_launch_checks(planted, monkeypatch)
        planted.config()
        cli = planted.home / launcher.CLI_REL
        cli.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        cli.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n")
        cli.chmod(0o755)
        real_check, seen = launch_gate.check_start, []
    
        def probing_check(**kw):
            fd = os.open(planted.dir / launch_gate.START_LOCK, os.O_RDWR)
            try:
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                seen.append("free")
            except BlockingIOError:
                seen.append("held")
            finally:
                os.close(fd)
            return real_check(**kw)
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(launch_gate, "check_start", probing_check)

planted = <conftest.Machine object at 0x7e64d88cf5c0>, sessions = ([], [])

    def test_a_full_set_of_slots_never_refuses_a_session(planted, sessions):
        planted.config()
        folder = planted.dir / "slots"
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        held = []
        for slot in (1, 2, 3):
            fd = os.open(folder / f"slot-{slot}.lock", os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT)
            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            (folder / f"slot-{slot}.json").write_text(json.dumps({"slot": slot, "pid": os.getpid(), "kind": "test"}))
            held.append(fd)
        try:
            from gov.resources.gate import read
            assert len(read(True)["slots"]["held"]) == 3

role = 'engineer', ticket = 'DAEO-zz90', target = 'group'

    def interrupted_launch(project, sandbox, cli, role, ticket, target):
        """Start ``gov launch``, wait until its session runs, send SIGINT, wait for the launcher to end.
    
        ``target`` is ``"group"`` (the signal goes to the launcher's process group,
        launcher and session alike, as Ctrl-C in a terminal sends it) or
        ``"launcher"`` (to the launcher's process alone, as ``kill -INT <pid>``).
        Returns ``(folder, return code)``: the session's temp folder, seen as a
        directory while the session ran.
        """
        ready = sandbox.elsewhere / "session-is-running.json"
        plan(cli, files={"work/left-behind.txt": "left by the session\n"}, ready=ready, wait=120)
        w46.plant_open_gate(sandbox)   # W2-20 S19 (DEC-1088 (5)(b)): the gate is planted open, its readings in the env
        script = cli_support.write_launcher(project, sandbox)
        process = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(script), "launch", role, ticket], cwd=str(project),
                                   env=w46.gov_environment(project, sandbox, cli), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
                                   preexec_fn=_own_group_with_default_interrupt)
        try:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + WAIT_S
            while not ready.is_file():
                if process.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    out, err = process.communicate(timeout=WAIT_S) if process.poll() is not None else ("", "")
>                   raise AssertionError(f"gov launch {role} {ticket} started no session within {WAIT_S:.0f} s "
                                         f"(exit code {process.poll()}): {(out + err).strip()[-400:]}")
E                                        AssertionError: gov launch engineer DAEO-zz90 started no session within 30 s (exit code 1): gov launch: LAUNCH_REFUSED: the role file template/governance/kernel/roles/engineer.md names the skill 'test-driven-development', which is not at .claude/skills/test-driven-development/SKILL.md

    def signalled_launch(project, sandbox, cli, signum, role=ENGINEER, ticket=ENGINEER_TICKET):
        """Start ``gov launch``, wait until its session runs, send ``signum`` to the launcher alone, wait for its end."""
        assert sys.platform.startswith("linux"), "the session's process is read from /proc"
        ready, pid_file, release = (sandbox.elsewhere / name for name in
                                    ("session-is-running.json", "session-process.json", "session-may-end"))
        plan(cli, files=LEFT, ready=ready, pid=pid_file, release=release, wait=SESSION_LIMIT_S)
        w46.plant_open_gate(sandbox)   # W2-20 S19 (DEC-1088 (5)(b)): the gate is planted open, its readings in the env
        before = listing(sandbox)
        script = cli_support.write_launcher(project, sandbox)
        # Files, not pipes: a session that lives on keeps a pipe open, and the launcher's end would not be seen.
        out_path, err_path = sandbox.elsewhere / "launch-stdout.txt", sandbox.elsewhere / "launch-stderr.txt"
        with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out_file, open(err_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as err_file:
            process = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(script), "launch", role, ticket], cwd=str(project),
                                       env=w46.gov_environment(project, sandbox, cli), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                       stdout=out_file, stderr=err_file, preexec_fn=_own_session_with_default_signals)
    
        def output():
            return out_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), err_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    
        session = None
        try:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + WAIT_S
            while not ready.is_file():
                if process.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() > deadline:
>                   raise AssertionError(f"gov launch {role} {ticket} started no session within {WAIT_S:.0f} s "
                                         f"(exit code {process.poll()}): {''.join(output()).strip()[-400:]}")

    def signalled_launch(project, sandbox, cli, signum, role=ENGINEER, ticket=ENGINEER_TICKET):
        """Start ``gov launch``, wait until its session runs, send ``signum`` to the launcher alone, wait for its end."""
        assert sys.platform.startswith("linux"), "the session's process is read from /proc"
        ready, pid_file, release = (sandbox.elsewhere / name for name in
                                    ("session-is-running.json", "session-process.json", "session-may-end"))
        plan(cli, files=LEFT, ready=ready, pid=pid_file, release=release, wait=SESSION_LIMIT_S)
        w46.plant_open_gate(sandbox)   # W2-20 S19 (DEC-1088 (5)(b)): the gate is planted open, its readings in the env
        before = listing(sandbox)
        script = cli_support.write_launcher(project, sandbox)
        # Files, not pipes: a session that lives on keeps a pipe open, and the launcher's end would not be seen.
        out_path, err_path = sandbox.elsewhere / "launch-stdout.txt", sandbox.elsewhere / "launch-stderr.txt"
        with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out_file, open(err_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as err_file:
            process = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(script), "launch", role, ticket], cwd=str(project),
                                       env=w46.gov_environment(project, sandbox, cli), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                       stdout=out_file, stderr=err_file, preexec_fn=_own_session_with_default_signals)
    
        def output():
            return out_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), err_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    
        session = None
        try:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + WAIT_S
            while not ready.is_file():
                if process.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() > deadline:
>                   raise AssertionError(f"gov launch {role} {ticket} started no session within {WAIT_S:.0f} s "

    def signalled_launch(project, sandbox, cli, signum, role=ENGINEER, ticket=ENGINEER_TICKET):
        """Start ``gov launch``, wait until its session runs, send ``signum`` to the launcher alone, wait for its end."""
        assert sys.platform.startswith("linux"), "the session's process is read from /proc"
        ready, pid_file, release = (sandbox.elsewhere / name for name in
                                    ("session-is-running.json", "session-process.json", "session-may-end"))
        plan(cli, files=LEFT, ready=ready, pid=pid_file, release=release, wait=SESSION_LIMIT_S)
        w46.plant_open_gate(sandbox)   # W2-20 S19 (DEC-1088 (5)(b)): the gate is planted open, its readings in the env
        before = listing(sandbox)
        script = cli_support.write_launcher(project, sandbox)
        # Files, not pipes: a session that lives on keeps a pipe open, and the launcher's end would not be seen.
        out_path, err_path = sandbox.elsewhere / "launch-stdout.txt", sandbox.elsewhere / "launch-stderr.txt"
        with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out_file, open(err_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as err_file:
            process = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(script), "launch", role, ticket], cwd=str(project),
                                       env=w46.gov_environment(project, sandbox, cli), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                       stdout=out_file, stderr=err_file, preexec_fn=_own_session_with_default_signals)
    
        def output():
            return out_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), err_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    
        session = None
        try:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + WAIT_S
            while not ready.is_file():
                if process.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() > deadline:
>                   raise AssertionError(f"gov launch {role} {ticket} started no session within {WAIT_S:.0f} s "
